# FOUNDRY_HANDOFF_202609271458 — READ FIRST on wake

First handoff for this seat.

## The one thing to carry forward

`rimworld/start_debug_game_ready` (and its sibling `rimworld/start_debug_game`) is
CURRENTLY UNUSABLE, full stop, on ANY mod list — reproduced identically on the 27-mod
minimal list AND a bare 9-mod list (Harmony + Core + all 5 DLC + RimBridgeServer +
one content mod, no interaction with the content mod). Real cause, read straight
from Player.log: `Verse.Root_Play.SetupForQuickTestPlay()` throws
`ArgumentOutOfRangeException` deep in `WorldPathGrid.RecalculateAllLayersPathCosts`
during `World.FinalizeInit`, so `Current.Game.World` never gets assigned and the
game is stuck at the main menu forever. This is vanilla engine code, not ours, not
RimBridge's own code (RimBridge just invokes the vanilla debug entry point). Full
account: `DEBUG_GAME_READY_WORLDUI_CRASH_1`. Every `prove_*.py` script and every
skill that opens with this call needs a different path until it's fixed — do not
spend bridge time re-discovering this by trying it again.

## What the owner should see

The quicktest crash above may or may not affect a NORMAL new-game world
generation (real planetCoverage, not quicktest-scale) — I could not test that
without risking a real world-gen run, and did not attempt it. If a fresh
campaign world is wanted soon, this is worth knowing about before that happens,
since the failure mode (world generation itself throwing mid-`FinalizeInit`,
for every biome's `WorldFeatureDef`) is not obviously quicktest-specific from the
stack trace alone — it's a guess, not a measurement, that it's scale-limited.

## What is half-done, and where it stops

- `MIASMA_SCUTTLER_PREDATION_1` — code shipped (RM_CompPlantPredator, built,
  deployed, validate_patch clean, jawa/get_defs confirms it's wired with
  tickerType Long so it will actually tick), left in `doing` because the live
  behavioral proof (spawn plant+scuttler, step ticks, confirm a kill+mote) is
  blocked by the quicktest crash above, not by anything in this item's own work;
  NEXT: once `DEBUG_GAME_READY_WORLDUI_CRASH_1` is fixed or worked around, run
  `python.exe src/RimMandrake/bridgetools/prove_plant_predator.py` (already
  written, just needs a working quicktest path) and close the item on what it
  shows.
- `DEBUG_GAME_READY_WORLDUI_CRASH_1` — root cause is identified and reproduced
  3x (see above), but no fix or Harmony workaround attempted; NEXT: decide
  whether this is worth a Harmony patch (bypass/guard the vanilla
  `WorldPathGrid.RecalculateAllLayersPathCosts` NRE) or is better left for
  Ludeon/mod authors to fix upstream, since every FOUNDRY quicktest-based
  workflow is blocked until one of those happens.
- `BMT_FAUNA_ABSORPTION_1` — its own "OWED: live verification" step (load the
  canonical save on the full 629 list, check for missing-mod/BMT_-name errors)
  is STILL not done; the abrupt shutdown this session interrupted it before any
  save load happened, and I did not re-attempt it after the quicktest-crash
  detour ate the rest of the bridge time available; NEXT: take the bridge,
  launch on the full 629 list (NOT minimal — this needs the real canonical
  save), load `CANONICAL_ASHKARR_START_2026-09-12`, and check for
  `Could not load reference` hits on any `BMT_`/`BiomesCore` name.

## Traps learned

- A 2026-09-25 ledger note falsely claimed a shade-follow commensal mechanism had shipped; it hadn't, corrected and re-blocked (see: DESERT_GLITTER_BIRDS_COMMENSALS_1).
- `rimworld/start_debug_game_ready` fails identically regardless of mod count or
  mod-list content — see "one thing to carry forward" above (filed:
  `DEBUG_GAME_READY_WORLDUI_CRASH_1`).
